forestry_resource_building_10 = {
	construction_time = construction_time_3_years
	can_construct_potential = {
		county = { has_potential_for_trade_good = trade_good_type:trade_good_wood }
	}
	can_construct_showing_failures_only = {
		building_forest_late_medieval_trigger = yes
		holding_district_level_4_trigger = yes
	}
	is_enabled = {
	}
	show_disabled = yes
	can_construct = {
	}
	cost_gold = resource_district_normal_tier_10_cost
	province_modifier = {
		building_slots_for_host_district = 4
		trade_good_wood_production_add = wood_tier_10_main_resource
	}
	province_government_modifier = {
		not_parameter = government_is_tribal
		monthly_woodland_tax = wood_tier_10_main_income
	}
	province_government_modifier = {
		parameter = government_is_tribal
		monthly_woodland_tax = wood_tier_10_main_income_tribal
	}
	province_terrain_modifier = {
		parameter = mountaineer_ruralism_mountain_production
		terrain = mountains
		trade_good_wood_production_mult = 0.25
	}
	market_modifier = {
		trade_good_iron_sell_price_mult = price_metal_building_1
	}

	province_culture_modifier = {
		parameter = industrious_productivity_bonus
		trade_good_wood_production_mult = 0.1
		monthly_woodland_tax = industrious_tradition_tax_bonus_high
	}

	trade_good_need = {
		trade_good = trade_good_iron #Felling Axes, tools
		amount = metal_resource_need

		can_be_filled_multiple_times = 3
		modifiers = {
			province_modifier = {
				trade_good_wood_production_mult = wood_multiplier_per_iron_need
				woodland_tax_mult = tax_mult_per_production_need
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
	}
	ai_value = {
		base = 10
		ai_resource_building_staple_need_modifier = { GOOD = trade_good_wood }
		ai_resource_building_consumer_modifier = { GOOD = trade_good_iron }
		directive_to_build_economy_modifier = yes
		ai_market_staple_shortage_modifier = { GOOD = trade_good_wood }
		ai_resource_district_upgrade_modifier = { LEVEL = 10 }
	}

	next_building = eu5_forestry_resource_building_11
}

grain_resource_building_10 = {

	construction_time = construction_time_3_years
	can_construct_showing_failures_only = {
		building_farmed_late_medieval_trigger = yes
		holding_district_level_4_trigger = yes
	}
	is_enabled = {
	}
	show_disabled = yes
	can_construct = {
	}
	cost_gold = resource_district_normal_tier_10_cost
	province_modifier = {
		building_slots_for_host_district = 4
		trade_good_grain_production_add = grain_tier_10_main_resource
	}
	province_government_modifier = {
		not_parameter = government_is_tribal
		monthly_agricultural_tax = grain_tier_10_main_income
	}
	province_government_modifier = {
		parameter = government_is_tribal
		monthly_agricultural_tax = grain_tier_10_main_income_tribal
	}
	province_terrain_modifier = {
		parameter = mountaineer_ruralism_mountain_production
		terrain = mountains
		trade_good_grain_production_mult = 0.25
	}
	market_modifier = {
		trade_good_iron_sell_price_mult = price_metal_building_1
		trade_category_beasts_of_burden_sell_price_mult = price_basic_building_1
	}

	province_culture_modifier = {
		parameter = industrious_productivity_bonus
		trade_good_grain_production_mult = 0.1
		monthly_agricultural_tax = industrious_tradition_tax_bonus_high
	}

	trade_good_need = {
		trade_good = trade_good_iron #Plows
		amount = metal_resource_need
		culture_parameter = heavy_plow
		modifiers = {
			province_modifier = {
				trade_good_grain_production_mult = grain_multiplier_per_iron_need
				agricultural_tax_mult = tax_mult_per_production_need
			}
		}
	}
	trade_good_need = {
		trade_good = trade_good_cattle #Oxen - Pulling the Plows
		amount = low_resource_district_need
		can_be_filled_multiple_times = 5
		not_culture_parameter = heavy_plow
		modifiers = {
			province_modifier = {
				trade_good_grain_production_mult = grain_multiplier_per_beast_need
				agricultural_tax_mult = tax_mult_per_production_need
			}
		}
	}

	trade_good_need = {
		trade_good_category = trade_category_beasts_of_burden #Oxen or Horses, once we have the Heavy Plow
		amount = low_resource_district_need
		can_be_filled_multiple_times = 5
		culture_parameter = heavy_plow
		modifiers = {
			province_modifier = {
				trade_good_grain_production_mult = grain_multiplier_per_beast_need
				agricultural_tax_mult = tax_mult_per_production_need
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
	}
	flag = agricultural_district_building
	flag = food_district_building
	ai_value = {
		base = 10
		ai_resource_building_need_category_modifier = { GOOD = trade_good_grain CATEGORY = trade_category_cereals }
		ai_resource_building_consumer_modifier = { GOOD = trade_good_iron }
		directive_to_build_economy_modifier = yes
		ai_market_category_shortage_modifier = { GOOD = trade_good_grain CATEGORY = trade_category_cereals }
		ai_resource_district_upgrade_modifier = { LEVEL = 10 }
	}


	next_building = eu5_grain_resource_building_11
}

	}

	can_construct = {
	}
	cost_gold = resource_district_normal_tier_10_cost
	province_modifier = {
		building_slots_for_host_district = 4
	}

		not_parameter = government_is_tribal
		monthly_maritime_tax = resource_tier_10_main_income
	}

		parameter = mountaineer_ruralism_mountain_production
		terrain = mountains
		trade_good_fish_production_mult = 0.25
	}
	#Fish

		trade_good = trade_good_fish
		trade_good_fish_production_add = tier_10_main_resource

		terrain = taiga
		trade_good_salt_sell_price_mult = northern_fish_salt_price_mult
	}

		parameter = northern_salt_curing
		trade_good_salt_sell_price_mult = northern_fish_salt_price_mult
	}
	trade_good_need = {
		trade_good = trade_good_salt
		amount = basic_resource_need
		terrain = taiga

				trade_good_fish_production_mult = fish_multiplier_per_salt_need
				maritime_tax_mult = tax_mult_per_production_need
			}
		}
	}
	trade_good_need = {
		trade_good = trade_good_salt
		amount = basic_resource_need
		culture_parameter = northern_salt_curing
		can_be_filled_multiple_times = 5
		modifiers = {
			province_modifier = {
				trade_good_fish_production_mult = fish_multiplier_per_salt_need
				maritime_tax_mult = tax_mult_per_production_need
			}
			market_modifier = {
				trade_good_fish_buy_price_mult = salted_fish_buy_price_mult
			}
		}
	}

	province_culture_modifier = {
		parameter = industrious_productivity_bonus
		trade_good_fish_production_mult = 0.1
		trade_good_gems_production_mult = 0.1
		monthly_maritime_tax = industrious_tradition_tax_bonus_high
	}

	province_culture_modifier = {
		parameter = fishermen_productivity_bonus

				trade_good_fish_production_mult = fish_multiplier_per_salt_need

			}
		}
	}

			}
			market_modifier = {
				trade_good_fish_buy_price_mult = salted_fish_buy_price_mult

		}

		trade_good_gems_production_mult = 0.1

			limit = {

			}
			building_farmed_high_medieval_trigger = yes
			holding_district_level_3_trigger = yes
		}
		trigger_else = {
			building_farmed_late_medieval_trigger = yes
			holding_district_level_4_trigger = yes
		}
	}

		terrain = mountains

	flag = agricultural_district_building

			}
		}
	}

		ai_resource_building_consumer_modifier = { GOOD = trade_good_wood }
		directive_to_build_economy_modifier = yes
		ai_market_category_shortage_modifier = { GOOD = trade_good_tea CATEGORY = trade_category_comforts }
		ai_resource_district_upgrade_modifier = { LEVEL = 10 }
	}

legume_resource_building_10 = {

	construction_time = construction_time_3_years
	can_construct_showing_failures_only = {
		building_farmed_late_medieval_trigger = yes
		holding_district_level_4_trigger = yes
	}
	is_enabled = {
	}
	show_disabled = yes
	can_construct = {
	}
	cost_gold = resource_district_normal_tier_10_cost
	province_modifier = {
		building_slots_for_host_district = 4
		trade_good_legumes_production_add = grain_tier_10_main_resource
	}
	province_government_modifier = {
		not_parameter = government_is_tribal
		monthly_agricultural_tax = grain_tier_10_main_income
	}
	province_government_modifier = {
		parameter = government_is_tribal
		monthly_agricultural_tax = grain_tier_10_main_income_tribal
	}
	province_terrain_modifier = {
		parameter = mountaineer_ruralism_mountain_production
		terrain = mountains
		trade_good_legumes_production_mult = 0.25
	}
	market_modifier = {
		trade_good_iron_sell_price_mult = price_metal_building_1
		trade_category_beasts_of_burden_sell_price_mult = price_basic_building_1
	}

	province_culture_modifier = {
		parameter = industrious_productivity_bonus
		trade_good_legumes_production_mult = 0.1
		monthly_agricultural_tax = industrious_tradition_tax_bonus_high
	}

	trade_good_need = {
		trade_good = trade_good_iron #Plows
		amount = metal_resource_need
		culture_parameter = heavy_plow
		modifiers = {
			province_modifier = {
				trade_good_legumes_production_mult = grain_multiplier_per_iron_need
				agricultural_tax_mult = tax_mult_per_production_need
			}
		}
	}
	trade_good_need = {
		trade_good = trade_good_cattle #Oxen - Pulling the Plows
		amount = low_resource_district_need
		can_be_filled_multiple_times = 5
		not_culture_parameter = heavy_plow
		modifiers = {
			province_modifier = {
				trade_good_legumes_production_mult = grain_multiplier_per_beast_need
				agricultural_tax_mult = tax_mult_per_production_need
			}
		}
	}

	trade_good_need = {
		trade_good_category = trade_category_beasts_of_burden #Oxen or Horses, once we have the Heavy Plow
		amount = low_resource_district_need
		can_be_filled_multiple_times = 5
		culture_parameter = heavy_plow
		modifiers = {
			province_modifier = {
				trade_good_legumes_production_mult = grain_multiplier_per_beast_need
				agricultural_tax_mult = tax_mult_per_production_need
			}
		}
	}
	on_complete = {
	}
	flag = agricultural_district_building
	flag = food_district_building
	ai_value = {
		base = 10
		ai_resource_building_need_category_modifier = { GOOD = trade_good_legumes CATEGORY = trade_category_cereals }
		ai_resource_building_consumer_modifier = { GOOD = trade_good_iron }
		directive_to_build_economy_modifier = yes
		ai_market_category_shortage_modifier = { GOOD = trade_good_legumes CATEGORY = trade_category_cereals }
		ai_resource_district_upgrade_modifier = { LEVEL = 10 }
	}


	next_building = eu5_legume_resource_building_11
